## pivot

### [ADJUSTED] Lizzie's ship faces the opposite way from the direction held: press RIGHT and her prop plane is drawn nosed up-LEFT, press LEFT and she is nosed up-RIGHT. Affects both the pv lean reel and the br edge-on twist reel on every stage using the normal player draw path.
- where: game.js:46832
- now: Input maps cleanly: assets/game.js:25983 sets dir=-1 for 'left' and +1 for 'right', 25988 ramps toward it and 25996 stores it, so left => player._bank<0, right => _bank>0. _shipFrameKey (assets/game.js:30396) resolves the frame through two per-pilot handedness tables at 30402-30403: `right=(SHIP_BANK_FLIP[pk] ? bank<0 : bank>0)` and `rightBr=(SHIP_TWIST_FLIP[pk] ? bank<0 : bank>0)`, consumed at 30419 (`'_br'+(rightBr?6:2)`) and 30420 (`ab<0.5?(right?'_pv3':'_pv1'):(right?'_pv4':'_pv0')`). Lizzie is in neither table (SHIP_BANK_FLIP={axel,decker,freezer,yuri} at 46832; SHIP_TWIST_FLIP={axel,decker,juggernaut} at 46866), so holding right resolves right=true -> _pv3/_pv4 and rightBr=true -> _br6. Her art is authored the mirrored way, like the pilots that do carry the flags: measured off assets/game/atlas/bof_player_ships_barrel_rolls.png her pv0/pv1 are nose-RIGHT (+0.024/+0.048) and pv3/pv4 nose-LEFT (-0.117/-0.113), and her br2 is nose-RIGHT / br6 nose-LEFT (+0.186/-0.186 by the topmost-ink metric; visually unambiguous at 1.1x). So _pv3/_pv4/_br6 are her LEFT-turn frames and the code hands them to right input. Why she was missed is recorded in the file: the drop 0903y table at assets/game.js:46850-46859 reads her lean as '(weak)' and her twist as br2 -.013 / br6 +.013, then concludes at 46862-46864 that 'her twist reads cleanly (br6 right) so she is out of this one too'. Those twist numbers are near-zero AND the wrong sign — the real separation is an order of magnitude larger and points the other way.
- FIX: Two one-word table edits, no change to _shipFrameKey. At assets/game.js:46832 make it `const SHIP_BANK_FLIP={axel:1, decker:1, freezer:1, yuri:1, lizzie:1};` and at assets/game.js:46866 make it `const SHIP_TWIST_FLIP={axel:1, decker:1, juggernaut:1, lizzie:1};`. Correct the stale lizzie row in the 0903y comment (assets/game.js:46859) while you are there so the next reader does not re-derive the same wrong conclusion.
- risk: SHIP_BANK_FLIP steers _shipThrRig (assets/game.js:30424), which looks the thruster row up by the resolved frame suffix, so the plume follows the hull — but lizzie is the one entry in THRUSTER_MOUNTS (assets/game.js:30918) with "flip": true, so her plume anchor is worth re-eyeballing at full bank. SHIP_TWIST_FLIP has a second consumer the report did not name: rollFrameKey at assets/game.js:8054 reverses the barrel-roll frame order on this flag, so adding lizzie also reverses her roll reel — almost certainly the desired direction given her br art is mirrored, but it is a visible change to a different animation and should be eyeballed. The twist half is invisible while she is docked: lzMountActive clamps her bank to +/-0.45 at assets/game.js:25995, under the TWIST threshold of 0.82 (assets/game.js:30404). Her _l/_r pair stays wrong regardless — its only live reader is drawRivalShip (assets/game.js:55713), which has no flip support; that is a separate fix, and it only bites if lizzie can be the rival.

### [ADJUSTED] In Gravity/space mode (stages 5 and 9) every pilot's ship yaws the wrong way: hold left and the craft noses right, hold right and it noses left. This path bypasses the per-pilot flip tables entirely, so it is wrong for all nine pilots.

## Stage 4 miniboss — OLIVE WARDEN (`olivewarden`): art frames, ammunition, turret muzzle flash, firing pattern

### [ADJUSTED] Mike: "dont use the projectiles your using for it now." For ~68% of its attack cycle the Warden is not firing machine guns at all — it sits near centre-screen and pumps out fans of big electric-blue LIGHTNING rounds from its nose/lower-centre mount.
- where: game.js:13825
- now: Stage 4's miniboss is `olivewarden` (SUBBOSS[4], assets/game.js:8245; table row SHIPBOSS.olivewarden 12214-12216). Its combat lives entirely in `stage4WarfareMiniTick` (13884). Two of its three modes — 'gate' (13899) and 'drones' (13903) — call `stage4WarfareWall` (13817), which at 13825 fires 8 of 11 columns with role `'spread'` from the single centre mount `'C'` (13822). `stage4WarfareShot`'s role->kind map at 13805 sends 'spread' to kind `s4rail`, and `drawStage4WarfareProjectile` at 33506 draws it as `s4w_spread_round_*` — a 96x96 electric-blue lightning bolt from the shared stage-4 warfare pack — at h=38 * S4H_SHOT_DRAW 1.45 = 55.1px. Line 13810 stamps `q._bfam='storm'` on every round, overriding the Warden's declared `proj:'legion'`. With S.t reset at 13773 on each mode change, the cycle is burst 3.60s + gate 2.40s + drones 5.20s = 11.20s, of which 7.60s (67.9%) is the blue fan. The gold machine-gun round it should fire already exists and is already warmed: role 'mg' -> `s4w_mg_round_*` (6 frames, 40x64, gold/white; drawn at 33504, registered 2191, warmed 12617 under a gate that names olivewarden at 12605).

The function is called only from 13901 and 13919 (both the mini's branches), so the change cannot reach the Storm Sovereign. Do not touch the role->kind map at 13805 — other roles depend on it.

### [ADJUSTED] Mike: "act like its shooting off dual machine gun spread turrets." It never does. Note also that both edits depend on `L`/`R` being correct mounts — they are (verified alpha 255 at plate (51,174)/(183,174)) — and that `stage4WarfareWall`'s early-return guard at 13821 is a leftover from when the boss shared the function; it is harmless but must not be relied on.

### [ADJUSTED] Purple MS-Paint triangle drawn ON every damaged Stage-5 enemy hull (also Stage 8/9 via the same function)

- now: drawSpaceDamage(e,frac,w,h,s9) at game.js:43304 returns only on frac>.66. Below that it paints three-to-five flat ctx.arc smoke blobs in rgba(18,21,30,.92)/rgba(37,40,54,.78) (43305) and then, at 43306, a hand-drawn three-point vector flame filled '#d248ff' (bright violet) for non-S9 callers and '#48eaff' for S9. It is called unconditionally from the S5SPACE hull draw at 43328 for all eleven Stage-5 hulls and from drawS9Void at 43552. This is NOT an XART.rdy fallback — it runs on top of correctly decoded authored art. Concurrently, the authored damage reel drawEnemyDamage (28298) runs on the same hulls from the world enemy loop at 54826, because its only bail-outs are `e._nef` (28302, set only for the stage 1-3 NEF rosters at 35317) and dmgTierFor returning null; DMG_TIER's first tier fires at f<0.65 (28203), which overlaps drawSpaceDamage's f<=0.66 almost exactly. So a damaged Stage-5/8/9 hull carries the authored nsd_diss/nsd_chim plume AND a procedural violet triangle plus grey circles simultaneously — precisely the 'two plumes from two different systems at two different scales' failure the comment at 28299-28301 was written to prevent.
- FIX: Minimal and directly on the symptom: in drawSpaceDamage (game.js:43304), delete the flame-triangle statement on line 43306 up to (not including) `ctx.restore();` — i.e. drop `const fy=...;ctx.fillStyle=s9?'#48eaff':'#d248ff';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-5,fy);ctx.lineTo(...);ctx.lineTo(6,fy);ctx.fill();` — leaving the smoke blobs and the restore intact. If instead the whole procedural overlay should defer to the authored reel, add as the first statement after the frac test: `if(typeof XART!=='undefined'&&XART.rdy('nsd_chim_0'))return;`. Do not touch the callers at 43328 or 43552.
- risk: Deleting only the triangle leaves the grey vector smoke double-plumed with the authored reel, so it fixes the purple but not the duplication. Taking the rdy-guard version instead: between 0.15 and 0.65 HP the authored tier is nsd_diss/nsd_chim (smoke only, no flame), so damaged Stage-5/8/9 hulls lose their fire cue until they drop under 0.15 where nxp_upward adds it back — both atlases exist, so that path does work. The guard also silences the overlay for any unit whose hull art decoded before fx_weather_1.png did, and it changes S9 as well as S5/S8 (the cyan variant is not the reported complaint).

Adding a glow re-enters drawMfx's bakeGlow path (32898) with a 9px blur, creating one baked sprite per (key,size,colour) triple and counting against _GLOW_CAP=512 at 32892; past that ceiling bakeGlow returns null (32904) and the round silently falls to the live-shadow path. Wiring the stage-4 atlas needs its frame counts/roles verified against combat_projectile_atlas_audit.json first.
